###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:7:5)


כְּלוֹמַר וְלֹא סַגִּי לֵיהּ בְּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא נִכְנַס לְתוֹךְ דִּבְרֵי חֲבֵרוֹ, אֲבָל יִתְמַהְמֵהַּ גַּם כֵּן עַד שֶׁיֵּדַע שֶׁיָּשִׁיב כַּהֲלָכָה:


["And is not impulsive in answering,"] means to say, that it is not enough that which "he does not interrupt the words of his fellow," but he also waits until he knows that he will answer as is proper.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:7:6)


וּתְמֵהַנִי דְּזוֹ מִדַּת כָּל אָדָם שֶׁלֹּא לְשַׁקֵּר, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן בִּפְסַק דִּין שֶׁיִּתְלֶה עַצְמוֹ בְּאִילָן גָּדוֹל כוּ' וְנִמְצָא מַכְשִׁיל הָרַבִּים, שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר שֶׁאֵין הַדִּין כָּךְ, גַּם הָרְאָיָה שֶׁהֵבִיא מֵאַנְשֵׁי חָרָן אֵינוֹ עִנְיָן לְנִדּוֹן זֶה. וְהָרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, וְלֹא יִתְפָּאֵר בְּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא יָדַע, וְהוּא אָמְרוֹ עַל מַה שֶּׁלֹּא שָׁמַע אוֹמֵר לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


And [Rabbi Bartenura's explanation on "about that which he has not heard [anything]" made me wonder, as this is the measure of every man - not to lie. All the more so in a legal decision, that he should make himself dependent on a [greater authority], etc. And it will come out that he will make the masses stumble, as it is possible that the law is not like this. And also the proof that he brought from the men of Charan is not [relevant] to this case at all. And Rambam explained, "And he should not glorify himself with that which he did not hear, and that is what it is saying, 'about that which he has not heard [anything], says, "I have not heard [anything]."' And see Tosafot Yom Tov.



### Mishna 8

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:1)


**גּוּפֵי כוּ'.** כְּלוֹמַר עֲבֵרוֹת חֲלוּקוֹת. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


**Categories (literally, bodies) of sin:** Which is to say, differing sins. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:2)


**מְעַשְּׂרִין.** מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁם כּוֹלֵל לְכָל הַמַּתָּנוֹת. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


**give tithes:** "Tithe (*maaser*)" is a general name for all of the gifts [to the Levites and priests]. And see Tosafot Yom Tov..


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:3)


**בַּצֹּרֶת כוּ'.** בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת דַּף ל"ב יָלְפִינַן לָהּ מִקְּרָא:


**A famine, etc.:** In Tractate Shabbat 32b, we learn it from scripture.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:4)


**הַחַלָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר אַף שֶׁלֹּא לִטֹּל אֶת הַחַלָּה. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


**the dough [offering]:** Which is to say, "also not set apart the dough [offering]." And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:5)


וְכֵיוָן שֶׁפָּשְׁעוּ הַבֵּית דִּין וְלֹא עָשׂוּ, לְפִיכָךְ דֶּבֶר בָּא לָעוֹלָם וְאֵינוֹ מַבְחִין בֵּין טוֹב לְרַע וְהָיָה כַּצַּדִּיק כָּרָשָׁע. אֲבָל לְשׁוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נִמְסְרוּ, לֹא מַשְׁמַע כֵּן, אֶלָּא כִּדְפֵרְשׁוּ הַמְפָרְשִׁים כְּגוֹן מִיתוֹת בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם וּכְרֵתוֹת, אִי נַמִּי שֶׁלֹּא בְּהַתְרָאָה אוֹ שֶׁבָּרַח הָעוֹבֵר:


And since the court sinned and did not do [a judgement], pestilence comes to the world, and it does not distinguish between the good and the bad, 'and it will be to the righteous one like the evil one.' But the expression, "that are not given over" does not imply this, but rather like the commentators explain - for example, [sins that result in] death penalties by the hand of the Heavens and excisions (*keritot*); and also (another explanation is) [sins that are committed] without warning or witnesses, or that the sinner escaped [such that the court cannot punish him].


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:6)


**שְׁבִיעִית.** שֶׁדִּינָם לְהַפְקִירָם, אִי נַמִּי לְבַעֲרָם לְגַמְרֵי וּכְמוֹ שֶׁתְּנַן בְּמִשְׁנָה ב' פֶּרֶק ט' דִּשְׁבִיעִית. וּלְפִיכָךְ מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה, שֶׁהַדֶּבֶר בָּא לָעוֹלָם שֶׁהוּא הֶפְקֵר בְּנֵי אָדָם שֶׁאֵין הַמַּלְאָךְ הַמַּשְׁחִית מַבְחִין. וְאִלּוּ חֶרֶב אֶפְשָׁר כִּי יְחַנֵּן קוֹלוֹ שָׁמוֹעַ יִשְׁמַע וְיַנִּיחֵהוּ הָאוֹיֵב. דֶּרֶךְ חַיִּים. וְהָנֵי תְּרֵי כֵּיוָן שֶׁדּוֹמִים בָּעֹנֶשׁ נֶחְשְׁבוּ לְחַד בְּמִנְיַן הַשִּׁבְעָה. וְכֵן בְּסָמוּךְ:


**the produce of the Sabbatical year:** to render them ownerless; and also (another explanation is) to completely dispose of them, as I wrote in Mishnah Sheviit 9:2. And therefore it is poetic justice that pestilence comes to the world, as it causes people to be 'ownerless,' as the destructive angel does not distinguish. But with the sword, it is possible that if his voice supplicates, the enemy will surely hear him and leave him - Derekh Chaim. And these two [sins] are considered one in the count of seven since they are similar in their punishment. And so [too] the adjacent [ones].


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:8:7)


**חֶרֶב כוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא (שָׁם) יָלְפִינַן לָהּ מִקְּרָא: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**[The] sword, etc.:** In the gemara (Shabbat 33a), we learn it from scripture.



### Mishna 9

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:1)


**חַיָּה רָעָה כוּ' גָּלוּת כוּ'.** כֻּלְּהוּ מַיְתֵי לְהוּ קְרָאֵי בַּגְּמָרָא שָׁם:


**Destructive animals, etc. Exile, etc.:** They bring verses [as proof-texts] for all of them in the gemara in Shabbat 33a.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:2)


**מִתְרַבֶּה.** שֶׁכַּאֲשֶׁר יִקְרֶה שֶׁיִּהְיֶה דֶּבֶר בְּאֵלּוּ הַפְּרָקִים יִתְרַבֶּה אָז בְּעָוֹן אֵלּוּ, לְפִי שֶׁיִּקְרֶה בָּהֶם תָּמִיד הֶעָוֹן אֵצֶל קְצָת הָאֲנָשִׁים, וְהִנֵּה הוּא מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה, כִּי הֶחְסִיר חַיּוּת הֶעָנִי וְגַם חָשַׁשׁ שֶׁכְּשֶׁיִּתֵּן שֶׁיֶּחְסַר לוֹ לַחְמוֹ כְּשֶׁיַּאֲרִיכוּ לוֹ חַיָּיו, לְכָךְ יַחְסְרוּ חַיָּיו וְתִשָּׁאֵר תְּבוּאָתוֹ לַאֲחֵרִים. וּמֵעַתָּה לֹא קָשְׁיָא עַל הַמִּשְׁנָה הַקּוֹדֶמֶת. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל:


**become more widespread, etc.:** As when it happens that there is pestilence in those periods of time, it becomes more widespread then, due to the sin of these [people]; as [this] sin always happens in these [time periods] with some people. And behold, it is poetic justice; as [such a man] took away from the life of the poor person and also was concerned that if he would give, it would take away from his [own] bread if his life is lengthened. Hence, his life will be taken from, and his produce will remain for others. And from now, there is no objection at all from the previous mishnah - Midrash Shmuel.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:9:3)


**וּבְמוֹצָאֵי הֶחָג.** כִּי בֶּחָג כְּבָר נִשְׁלַם עֵסֶק הָאֲדָמָה, וּמִי שֶׁנָּתַן אֵלּוּ הַחֻקִּים נְתָנָם, וּמִי שֶׁלֹּא נְתָנָם גְּזָלָם. הָרַמְבַּ"ם: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**and after the holiday (Sukkot):** As at the holiday, occupation with the earth (agricultural pursuits) is already finished. And the one that gave these obligations, gave them; and the one who did not give them, stole them - Rambam.



### Mishna 10

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:1)


**שֶׁלִּי כוּ'.** לֹא בְּנוֹתְנֵי צְדָקָה אַיְרִינַן, דְּהַנְהוּ מַתְנְיָין לְקַמָּן מִשְׁנָה י"ג, אֶלָּא בַּהֲנָאָה דְּעָלְמָא:


**"What is mine, etc.":** We are not speaking about givers of charity - as those we teach later [in] Mishnah 13 - but rather about benefit in general.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:2)


וְדַוְקָא שֶׁהוּא רוֹצֶה לֵהָנוֹת מִנִּכְסֵי אֲחֵרִים מִדַּעְתָּן. דְּאִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעְתָּן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרוֹצֶה גַּם לְהַנּוֹתָם הוּא גַּזְלָן גָּמוּר וְאֵין לְךָ רָשָׁע גָּדוֹל מִזֶּה. וְלֹא דִּבְּרוּ חֲזַ"ל אֶלָּא בְּדֵעוֹת, וְלֹא עַל הַגַּנָּב וְהַגַּזְלָן שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל:


And [when the mishnah speaks about mutual benefit, it is] specifically if he wants to benefit from the possessions of others with their knowledge. As if it is without their knowledge - even though he wants to also benefit them - he is a total thief and you can have no greater evildoer than this. And the sages, may their memory be blessed, only spoke about [normative] temperaments and they did not speak about the robber and the thief, who is an evildoer - Midrash Shmuel.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:10:3)


**חָסִיד.** לֹא שֶׁמְּבַזְבֵּז כָּל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ. שֶׁהֲרֵי אָמְרוּ הַמְּבַזְבֵּז אַל יְבַזְבֵּז יוֹתֵר מֵחֹמֶשׁ, שֶׁלֹּא יְבַזְבֵּז יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי וְיִהְיֶה הוּא בְּהֶכְרֵחַ מֻטָּל עַל הַבְּרִיּוֹת. אֶלָּא בִּמְוַתֵּר כְּפִי הָרָאוּי אַיְרִינַן. דֶּרֶךְ חַיִּים: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**a pious person:** Not that he scatters everything that he has, as behold, they said, "The one that scatters, let him not scatter more than a fifth" - so that he not scatter more than [that which is] enough and he perforce become dependent on the creatures. But rather we are talking about one who foregoes according to what is fit - Derekh Chaim.



### Mishna 11

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:11:1)


**קָשֶׁה כוּ'.** אֲבָל שֶׁלֹּא יִכְעַס כְּלָל לֹא נִמְצָא בְּשׁוּם דֵּעָה. כִּי מִי לָנוּ גָּדוֹל בַּעֲנָוָה כְּמֹשֶׁה רַבֵּנוּ עָלָיו הַשָּׁלוֹם וְנֶאֱמַר בּוֹ וַיִּקְצֹף וְגוֹ' וְכוּ'. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל:


**[A person who is] hard, etc.:** But one who does not get angry at all is not found in any disposition. As who is greater for us in humility than Moshe, our teacher, - peace be upon him - and about him it is said (Numbers 31:14), "And Moshe got angry, etc.," etc. - Midrash Shmuel.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:11:2)


**רָשָׁע.** דְּאָמַר מַר לֹא תִּרְתַּח וְלֹא תֶּחֱטָא. רַשִׁ"י. וְדֶרֶךְ חַיִּים מְפָרֵשׁ, כִּי הָא דְּאָמְרִינַן בִּנְדָרִים כָּל הַכּוֹעֵס בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁעֲוֹנוֹתָיו מְרֻבִּין, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר אִישׁ אַף יְגָרֶה מָדוֹן וּבַעַל חֵמָה רַב פָּשַׁע: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**an evildoer:** As the master said, "Don't be angry, and don't sin" - Rashi. And Derekh Chaim explains that it is like what we say in Nedarim 22b, "Any person who gets angry, it is known that his sins are greater [than his merits], as is states, '(A man of anger stirs up strife;) and a wrathful man much transgression.'"



### Mishna 12

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:12:1)


וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר בְּסִיַּעְתָּא דִּשְׁמַיָּא לְאוֹקוֹמֵי גִּרְסָתוֹ כְּשֶׁיִּרְאַת חֶטְאוֹ קוֹדֶמֶת לְחָכְמָתוֹ, מִי שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ לִכְלַל מִדָּה זוֹ אֵין רָאוּי לְהִקָּרֵא רָשָׁע כֵּיוָן שֶׁחֶסְרוֹן בְּרִיָּתוֹ הֱבִיאוֹ לִידֵי כָּךְ: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


And even though it is possible with the help of Heaven to raise his study, if his fear of sin precedes his wisdom; it is not fitting for someone who has not reached inclusion of this trait to be called an evildoer, as the disadvantage in his creation led him to this.



### Mishna 13

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:13:1)


וְעֵינוֹ רָעָה בְּשֶׁלּוֹ לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ אַבִּירוּת לֵב. וְאֵינוֹ דּוֹמֶה לְעֵינוֹ רָעָה בְּשֶׁל אֲחֵרִים. רַשִׁ"י. אֶלָּא כְּלוֹמַר כְּעַיִן רָעָה דְּמַתְנִיתִין י"ט, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִסְתַּפֵּק בְּשֶׁלּוֹ וְחוֹמֵד בְּמַה שֶּׁרָאוּי אֶל הֶעָנִי. וְקָשֶׁה דְּאִם כֵּן רָשָׁע הוּא שֶׁעוֹבֵר עַל לֹא תְאַמֵּץ אֶת לְבָבְךָ. וְאֶפְשָׁר דְּהָכָא אַיְרֵי בְּעִנְיָן שֶׁאֵין מַחְסוֹר לֶעָנִי וְנִמְצָא שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְאַמֵּץ לְבָבוֹ מִתֵּת לֶעָנִי דֵּי מַחְסוֹרוֹ, וְאִלּוּ לֹא הָיָה מִי שֶׁיִּתֵּן הָיָה הוּא נוֹתֵן וּרְצוֹנוֹ לִתֵּן כְּשֶׁיִּמְצָא עָנִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ נוֹתְנִים, וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם אַבִּירוּת לֵב הוּא. וְהַשְׁתָּא אָתֵי שַׁפִּיר דְּיִתֵּן וְיִתְּנוּ אֲחֵרִים חָסִיד, דְּקָשֶׁה, שֶׁכֵּן הוּא מִדַּת כָּל אָדָם, אֶלָּא דְּהָכָא בְּעָנִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ דֵּי סִפּוּקוֹ בִּנְתִינַת אֶחָד מֵהֶם, וְהוּא רוֹצֶה שֶׁיִּתֵּן וְיִתְּנוּ כְּדֵי לְמַלְּאוֹת סִפּוּקוֹ בְּשֶׁפַע, לְאִישׁ כָּזֶה רָאוּי לְהִקָּרֵא חָסִיד. כֵּן נִרְאֶה לִי: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


And "he has an evil eye regarding himself" because he has brazenness of the heart. And it is not similar to "he has an evil eye regarding others" - Rashi. But rather it is to say, it is like the "evil eye" in Mishnah 19, [of the one] who does not suffice with what is his and covets that which is fitting for the poor person. And there is an objection: If so, he is an evildoer, as he transgresses against "do not harden your heart." And it is possible that here we are speaking about the [case] that there is no lack to the poor person and it comes out that he does not harden his heart from giving to the poor person what he lacks. And if [the poor person] did not have someone to give to him, he would have given. And his desire is to give when he finds a poor person who has no givers; and nonetheless, this is brazenness of heart. And now it comes out well that "[to] give and [that] others give -- [that's a] pious person"; as there is an objection that such is the temperament of every man. But rather here it is concerning a poor person who has what he needs, through the giving of one of them. But he wants that he give and [also that others] give - so as to fill his requirements with plenty. It is fitting that such a person be called a pious person. So does it appear to me.



### Mishna 14

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:14:1)


יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ דְהָכִי קָאָמַר, אִי נַמִּי לוֹמֵד וְאֵינוֹ מֵבִין. וְיֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ, דְּהָכִי קָאָמַר, וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹנֶה וְאֵינוֹ לוֹמֵד וְלֹא מֵבִין:


And one can explain that this is what [Rabbi Bartenura] said: And also he [goes to] study and does not understand. And one can [also] explain that this is what he said: And he does not review and he does not study and he does not understand.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:14:2)


**חָסִיד.** שֶׁמַּשְׁפִּיל עַצְמוֹ וּמַרְאֶה כְּאִלּוּ הוּא צָרִיךְ לַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**a pious person:** As he lowers himself and acts as if he needs the ones sitting in the House of Study - Midrash Shmuel. And see Tosafot Yom Tov



### Mishna 15

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:15:1)


**סְפוֹג.** צֶמֶר הַיָּם. הָרַמְבַּ"ם: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**the sponge:** Sea wool - Rambam.



### Mishna 16

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:16:1)


**בְּדָבָר.** גִּרְסַת הָרַ"ב בְּדָבָר בָּטֵל, בָּטֵל דָּבָר כוּ':


**On something:** The textual variant of Rabbi Bartenura is "on a transient thing, when that thing perishes, etc."


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:16:2)


**אַמְנוֹן וְתָמָר.** לְדֻגְמָא בְּעָלְמָא נַקְטֵיהּ. וּלְפִיכָךְ לֹא קָשְׁיָא רֵישָׁא לְסֵיפָא: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**Amnon and Tamar:** It took it as a general example. And therefore there is no objection from the beginning [of the mishnah] against the end.



### Mishna 17

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:17:1)


**הִלֵּל וְשַׁמַּאי.** לְפִי שֶׁתַּלְמִידֵיהֶם לֹא שִׁמְּשׁוּ כָּל צָרְכָּן וּמֵהֶם וְאֵילָךְ רָבְתָה הַמַּחֲלֹקֶת בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, לְפִיכָךְ נַקְטִינְהוּ לְדֻגְמָא. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי נַמִּי לֹא קָשְׁיָא רֵישָׁא לְסֵיפָא. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


**Hillel and Shammai:** Because their students did not serve [in their studies] to their fullest and from them onward, disagreement grew in Israel; therefore it took them for an example. And because of this, there is also no objection from the beginning [of the mishnah] against the end. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:17:2)


**קֹרַח כוּ'.** לֹא הִזְכִּיר צַד הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁהֵם מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן כְּמוֹ שֶׁזָּכַר בַּחֲלֻקָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה שְׁנֵי הַצְּדָדִים, לְפִי שֶׁבְּכָאן אֵינָם שָׁוִים, שֶׁמֹּשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן כַּוָּנָתָם לַשָּׁמַיִם הָיְתָה, וְלֹא הָיְתָה בָּהֶם שׁוּם בְּחִינָה שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**Korach, etc.:** It did not mention the second side of the argument - which is Moshe and Aaron - as it mentioned the two sides in the first [argument]; as here they are not equal. As Moshe and Aaron's intentions were for the sake of Heaven, and there was no aspect in them that was not for the sake of Heaven's name - Midrash Shmuel.



### Mishna 18

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:18:1)


בַּגְּמָרָא מַפִּיק לְהוּ מִקְּרָאֵי. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


In the gemara, it derives it from verses. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:18:2)


**אֵין מַסְפִּיקִין כוּ'.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין מְסַיְּעִין לוֹ כוּ'. מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן לִשְׁאָר עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה כְּשֶׁיִּתֵּן אֶל לִבּוֹ לָשׁוּב וַדַּאי דַּהֲוֵי בִּכְלָל בָּא לִטָּהֵר מְסַיְּעִין לוֹ. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**is not given, etc.:** meaning to say, he is not helped, etc.; which is not the case with other perpetrators of sin: When [they] put into [their] hearts to repent, they are certainly in the category of '[one who] comes to purify, he is aided.' And see Tosafot Yom Tov.



### Mishna 19

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:1)


שֶׁמִּצְוֹת בְּנֵי אָדָם אֵינָם רַק לְפִי דַּעְתָּם. כִּי הַטּוֹבִים לֹא יְצַוּוּ בְּרַע, אֲבָל יַזְהִירוּ מִמֶּנּוּ. וְהַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר הֵן הֵנָּה הָיוּ לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּדְבַר בִּלְעָם. הָרַמְבַּ"ם:


As the commandments of people are only according to their opinions, since the good will not command to [do] evil, but [rather] to guard from it. And the verse states (Numbers 31:16), "They were the ones that were against the Children of Israel according to the counsel of Bilaam" - Rambam.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:2)


**בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה.** זֶה עַל דֶּרֶךְ יְגִיעַ כַּפֶּיךָ כִּי תֹאכֵל אַשְׁרֶיךָ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, כְּפֵרוּשׁ רַשִׁ"י בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ד'. דְּאִי לֹא תֵימָא הָכִי, הֲרֵי כַּמָּה מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ עָלָיו הַשָּׁלוֹם הָרְעֵבִים וּצְמֵאִים בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, אֶלָּא לְפִי שֶׁהֵם מִסְתַּפְּקִין וְאֵינָם צְרִיכִים לַבְּרִיּוֹת נִקְרְאוּ אוֹכְלִים בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה. וְעוֹד יֵשׁ לוֹמַר שֶׁשּׁוּרַת הַדִּין הוּא אוֹמֵר, אֲבָל אֵל דֵּעוֹת ה' וְלוֹ נִתְכְּנוּ עֲלִילוֹת לְהַנְחִילוֹ יוֹתֵר שָׂכָר בָּעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**"in this world":** This is in the way [of] "When you eat [from] the work of your hands, you will be happy" - in this world; as Rashi explained at the beginning of Chapter 4. As if you don't say like that, behold how many students of Abraham, our father - peace be upon him - are hungry and thirsty in this world. But rather, since they suffice and do not depend upon the creatures, they are called eaters in this world. And one could also say [another explanation is] that it is saying [here only according to] the measure of justice, but 'the Lord is the God of knowledge and by Him are actions measured' to have them inherit more [instead of this] in the world to come.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:3)


**יוֹרְשִׁין גֵּיהִנֹּם.** בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה. כִּדְאָמַר [יוֹמָא דַּף עב] לֹא תִירְתוּ תַּרְתֵּי גֵּיהִנֹּם. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל: וְהוּא גַּם כֵּן אִישׁ מִרְמָה בַּעֲשׂוֹתוֹ תַּחְבּוּלוֹת לִפְעֻלּוֹת הָרַע. הָרַמְבַּ"ם: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**inherit *Gehinnom* (Purgatory):** in this world, as it said (Yoma 72b), "Do not inherit two [doses of] *Gehinnom*" - Midrash Shmuel.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:19:4)


וְהוּא גַּם כֵּן אִישׁ מִרְמָה בַּעֲשׂוֹתוֹ תַּחְבּוּלוֹת לִפְעֻלּוֹת הָרַע. הָרַמְבַּ"ם:


And he is also a "man of deceit," in his doing machinations [to accomplish] evil - Rambam



### Mishna 20

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:1)


**עַז פָּנִים לְגֵיהִנֹּם.** שֶׁמֵּעִיז פָּנָיו שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹן אָבִיו שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם:


**"[The] brazen-faced [are bound] for *Gehinnom* (Purgatory)":** as he makes his face brazen not to do the will of his Father in heaven.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:2)


וְנֶאֱמַר יָשׁוּבוּ רְשָׁעִים לִשְׁאוֹלָה:


And it is stated, "May the evildoers return to the grave."


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:20:3)


**וּבֹשֶׁת פָּנִים לְגַן עֵדֶן.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ חֲזַ"ל כָּל אָדָם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בֹּשֶׁת פָּנִים לֹא בִּמְהֵרָה הוּא חוֹטֵא, כְּדִכְתִיב וּבַעֲבוּר תִּהְיֶה יִרְאָתוֹ עַל פְּנֵיכֶם לְבִלְתִּי תֶחֱטָאוּ. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**and [the] shamefaced [are bound] for the Garden of Eden:** As the sages, of blessed memory, said, "Any man that has shamefacedness will not quickly sin, as it is written (Exodus 20:17), 'and in order that His fear will be on your faces, so that you not sin'" - Midrash Shmuel.



### Mishna 21

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:1)


כְּשֶׁתַּחְשֹׁב שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה שֶׁעָשָׂה יַעֲקֹב עִם לָבָן אַחַר שֶׁנָּשָׂא אֶת לֵאָה וְיָלְדָה לוֹ אַחַר שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים בְּקֵרוּב שֶׁעָלוּ בְּהֵרָיוֹן לִשְׁלֹשָׁה הָאַחִים רְאוּבֵן שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן שִׁבְעָה חֳדָשִׁים לְכָל אֶחָד, נִמְצָא לֵוִי בֶּן אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה כְּשֶׁיָּצְאוּ מִשָּׁם, הוֹסֵיף עֲלֵיהֶם שִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים שֶׁעָשָׂה בַּדֶּרֶךְ וּשְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר חֳדָשִׁים שֶׁעָשָׂה בְּסֻכּוֹת קַיִץ וְחֹרֶף וְקַיִץ שֶׁהֵם שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים, הֲרֵי לֵוִי בֶּן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה בְּלֶכְתָּם לִשְׁכֶם. וְנִקְרָא אִישׁ. רַשִׁ"י. וּבַחֻמָּשׁ פָּרָשַׁת וַיֵּשֶׁב בְּפָסוּק וַיִּתְאַבֵּל שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ רַשִׁ"י שִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים בְּבֵית אֵל שֶׁהוּא אַחַר מַעֲשֵׂה שְׁכֶם, לֹא דִּיֵּק:


When you calculate the thirteen years that Yaakov [spent] with Lavan after he married Leah, and she gave birth to [Levi] after about two years - as the pregnancies of the three brothers, Reuven, Shimon and Levi, add up to seven months for each one - it comes out that Levi was eleven years old when they left from there. Add upon them six months that he [spent] on the way and eighteen months that he [spent] in Sukkot, summer, winter and summer - which are two years - behold, Levi is thirteen years of age in their going to Shekhem, and he is [still] called a man - Rashi. And he was not precise in his commentary on the Torah, Parshat Vayeshev, on the verse (Genesis 37:34), "and he mourned for his son many days," as [there] he explained that it was six months in Beit El, which is after the story of Shekhem.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:2)


לֹא נִמְצָא רַק שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה. וּבְפֵרוּשׁ רַשִׁ"י דִּשְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה פְּעָמִים כְּתִיב אָדָם עַד כִּי מֵאִישׁ לֻקֳּחָה זֹאת. וְצָרִיךְ לוֹמַר דְּאִישׁ נַמִּי מִן הַמִּנְיָן:


There are only sixteen [mentions of the word,] 'man' found. And in the explanation of Rashi, [it is found] that 'man' is written eighteen times until "as from the person (*eesh*) was this taken." To here [are this words]. And one must say that [the word,] person is also in the count.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:3)


בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבָּה פָּרָשַׁת קֹרַח. וְנִרְאֶה לִי מִדְּאַשְׁכְּחַן בְּמֵתֵי מִדְבָּר שֶׁלֹּא נֶעֶנְשׁוּ בְּפָחוֹת מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים:


[Rabbi Bartenura's second explanation is] in Midrash Rabbah, Parshat Korach. And it appears to me that it is from that which we find by those that died in the wilderness that they were not punished [when] less than twenty years old.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:4)


גְּמָרָא. וְנִרְאֶה לִי דִּרְצוֹנוֹ לוֹמַר דְּיִשְׂרָאֵל בִּשְׁעַת מַתַּן תּוֹרָה גֵּרִים הָיוּ, וְגֵר כְּקָטָן שֶׁנּוֹלַד דָּמֵי. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


Gemara. And it appears to me that it wants to say that Israel at the time of the giving of the Torah were converts and a convert is similar to an infant that was [just] born. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:21:5)


**לַזִּקְנָה.** וְיֶשׁ סְפָרִים דְּגַרְסֵי לַחָכְמָה. **בִּישִׁישִׁים חָכְמָה** נוֹטָרִיקוֹן בֶּן שִׁשִּׁים. רִיטְבָ"א. וְאַף לְגִרְסָתֵנוּ זָקֵן זֶה שֶׁקָּנָה חָכְמָה. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


"In elders (*beyishishim*) there is wisdom," [the first word of which is a type of] acronym for one of sixty (*ben shishim*) - Ritva. And even according to our textual variant, an elder (*zaken*) is *zeh she'kanah chocmah* (the one who acquired wisdom) - Midrash Shmuel. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.



### Mishna 22

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:1)


**בֶּן בַּג בַּג.** יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ שֶׁלֹּא הֶאֱרִיךְ יָמִים. וְכֵן בֶּן הֵא הֵא. וְהָרַשְׁבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁגֵּרִים הָיוּ. וְהֵם בְּנֵי אַבְרָהָם וְשָׂרָה שֶׁנִּתּוֹסְפוּ ה' עַל שְׁמוֹתָם, וְכָל הַגֵּרִים בְּנֵיהֶם נִקְרְאוּ. וּבַג גִּימַטְרִיָּא ה'. וּקְרָאָם זֶה מְשֻׁנֶּה מִזֶּה כְּדֵי לְהַכִּיר בֵּין זֶה לְזֶה. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל:


**Ben Bag Bag:** [That he is known by his patrimonial (Ben x)] can be explained as because he did not have length of days. But Rashbam explained that they were converts; and they are children of Abraham and Sarah, who had a [letter], *hey* added onto their names - and all converts are called their children. And the numerical equivalent of [the letters in] Bag is five (*hey*). And they called [Ben Bag Bag and Ben Hey Hey], one differently than the other, in order to recognize between one and the other - Midrash Shmuel.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:2)


וּלְפִי שֶׁדִּבְרֵי תּוֹרָה הֵן צֹרֶךְ גָּדוֹל לִבְנֵי אָדָם, הָיָה אוֹמֵר בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי שֶׁהָיוּ הַכֹּל מַכִּירִין בּוֹ כְּשֶׁעָלוּ מִבָּבֶל. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


And because words of Torah are a great need for people, [Ben Bag Bag] would say this in the Aramaic language, since they all understood it when they ascended [to Israel] from Babylonia - Midrash Shmuel. And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:3)


**וַהֲפֹךְ.** כֶּפֶל לָשׁוֹן לְחַזֵּק וּלְהוֹרוֹת עַל הַתְּמִידוּת וְהַהַפְלָגָה. וְעִנְיָן הַהִפּוּךְ עַצְמָהּ הוּא חֲזָרַת הַלִּמּוּד:


**"and search":** The repetitive language is to strengthen and teach about [its] constancy and emphasis. And the matter of searching, itself, is the review of learning.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:4)


**תֶּחֱזֵי.** רְצוֹנוֹ לוֹמַר הָאֱמֶת. פֵּרוּשׁ, וְתִרְאֶה הָאֱמֶת בְּעֵין הַשֵּׂכֶל. כְּתַרְגּוּם וַיַּרְא, וַחֲזָא. וְעַיֵּן תּוֹסְפוֹת יוֹם טוֹב:


**"should you look (*techeze*)":** ["It"] means to say the truth. The explanation is, and you should see the truth with the eye of the intellect, like the [Aramaic] translation of "and He saw," "*vachaza*." And see Tosafot Yom Tov.


###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:22:5)


**טוֹבָה הֵימֶנָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאמַר שֶׁבְּלִמּוּד חָכְמַת יְוָנִית בְּהֶכְרַח אֶלְמֹד מֵהֶם מְדִינִיּוּת וְהַנְהָגָה וּמִדּוֹת טוֹבוֹת, לְהָכִי קָאָמַר שֶׁאֵין לְךָ מִדָּה טוֹבָה הֵימֶנָּה, כִּי כָּל הַמִּדּוֹת טוֹבוֹת עֲרוּכוֹת בַּכֹּל וּשְׁמוּרוֹת בָּהּ. מִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


**"greater than it":** such that you not say that through the learning of Greek wisdom, "perforce I will learn from them politics and administration and virtues (good characteristics)." For this [reason], it said that "there is no characteristic greater than it," as all of the virtues are set up in all [that is in it] and preserved in it - Midrash Shmuel.



### Mishna 23

###### Ikar Tosafot Yom Tov on Pirkei Avot 5:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Ikar%20Tosafot%20Yom%20Tov%20on%20Pirkei%20Avot/r/5:23:1)


וְזֶה בִּשְׂכַר הַצַּעַר וְהַטֹּרַח עַצְמוֹ, שֶׁאִם הוּא מְרֻבֶּה שְׂכָרוֹ מְרֻבֶּה. אֲבָל שְׂכַר מִצְוֹת עַצְמָן אִי אַתָּה יוֹדֵעַ מַתַּן שְׂכָרָן. כְּדִתְנַן בְּרֵישׁ פֶּרֶק ב'. דֶּרֶךְ חַיִּים: 
(עיקר תוי"ט).


And this is about the reward for the pain and bother itself - that if it is great, its reward will be great. But [regarding] the reward for the [performance of the] commandments themselves, you do not know the gift of their reward, as we learned at the beginning of Chapter 2 - Derekh Chaim.
